<!-- SINTETIZADOR DE AUDIO MINAS VIP -->
<script>
    const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    let minesAudioCtx = null;

    function getAudioCtx() {
        if (!minesAudioCtx) minesAudioCtx = new AudioCtx();
        if (minesAudioCtx.state === 'suspended') minesAudioCtx.resume();
        return minesAudioCtx;
    }

    // Sonido brillante al descubrir Gema
    function playGemSound(pitchStep = 0) {
        try {
            const ctx = getAudioCtx();
            const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = ctx.createGain();
            osc.type = 'sine';
            const baseFreq = 880 + (pitchStep * 60);
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(baseFreq, ctx.currentTime);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(baseFreq * 1.5, ctx.currentTime + 0.15);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, ctx.currentTime);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + 0.25);
            osc.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(ctx.destination);
            osc.start();
            osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.25);
        } catch(e) {}
    }

    // Sonido sordo de Explosión / Bomba
    function playBombSound() {
        try {
            const ctx = getAudioCtx();
            const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = ctx.createGain();
            osc.type = 'sawtooth';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(140, ctx.currentTime);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(30, ctx.currentTime + 0.35);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.4, ctx.currentTime);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.35);
            osc.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(ctx.destination);
            osc.start();
            osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.35);
        } catch(e) {}
    }

    // Tintineo de monedas al cobrar
    function playCoins() {
        try {
            const ctx = getAudioCtx();
            [987, 1318, 1567, 2093].forEach((freq, idx) => {
                const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
                const gain = ctx.createGain();
                osc.type = 'sine';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, ctx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08));
                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, ctx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08));
                gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08) + 0.25);
                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(ctx.destination);
                osc.start(ctx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08));
                osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08) + 0.25);
            });
        } catch(e) {}
    }
</script>

<script>
    let isGameRunning = {{ in_game|yesno:'true,false' }};
    let revealedGems = {{ current_revealed|safe }};

    function setMinesCount(count) {
        if (isGameRunning) return;
        document.getElementById('selected-mines-count').value = count;
        [1, 3, 5, 10].forEach(c => {
            const btn = document.getElementById(`btn-m-${c}`);
            if (btn) {
                if (c === count) {
                    btn.className = "py-2 rounded-xl border-2 border-yellow-400 bg-yellow-500/20 text-xs font-mono font-bold text-yellow-300 shadow";
                } else {
                    btn.className = "py-2 rounded-xl border border-purple-500/40 bg-black/60 text-xs font-mono font-bold text-stone-300";
                }
            }
        });
    }

    function setBetAmount(val) {
        if (!isGameRunning) document.getElementById('mines-bet-input').value = val;
    }

    async function startMinesGame() {
        const betVal = parseInt(document.getElementById('mines-bet-input').value, 10);
        const minesCount = parseInt(document.getElementById('selected-mines-count').value, 10);
        const tgId = (typeof window.getGlobalTgId === 'function') ? window.getGlobalTgId() : '{{ tg_id }}';

        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('tg_id', tgId);
        formData.append('action', 'start');
        formData.append('bet_amount', betVal);
        formData.append('mines_count', minesCount);
        formData.append('ajax', '1');
        formData.append('csrfmiddlewaretoken', '{{ csrf_token }}');

        try {
            const resp = await fetch("{% url 'economy:mines' %}", {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData,
                headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' }
            });
            const data = await resp.json();

            if (!data.success) {
                alert(data.error);
                return;
            }

            isGameRunning = true;
            revealedGems = [];

            // Actualizar saldo
            document.getElementById('mines-balance').innerText = data.nuevo_saldo;

            // Resetear casilleros visualmente
            for (let i = 0; i < 25; i++) {
                const tile = document.getElementById(`tile-${i}`);
                const hiddenCover = tile.querySelector('.tile-hidden');
                tile.className = "mine-tile relative rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-[#281340] to-[#120520] border border-vip-gold/30 flex items-center justify-center text-xl shadow hover:border-vip-gold active:scale-95 transition-all aspect-square select-none";
                hiddenCover.classList.remove('hidden');
                tile.querySelector('.tile-icon').innerText = '💎';
            }

            document.getElementById('mult-val').innerText = '1.00';
            document.getElementById('cashout-val').innerText = data.bet;
            document.getElementById('cashout-btn').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('status-tag').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('status-label').innerText = 'En juego';
            document.getElementById('mines-setup-controls').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('mines-result-box').innerHTML = '<span class="text-xs text-yellow-300 font-bold animate-pulse">¡Ronda iniciada! Toca un cofre...</span>';

            try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.impactOccurred('medium'); } catch(e) {}

        } catch(e) {
            alert("Error de conexión al iniciar.");
        }
    }

    async function revealTile(index) {
        if (!isGameRunning || revealedGems.includes(index)) return;

        const tgId = (typeof window.getGlobalTgId === 'function') ? window.getGlobalTgId() : '{{ tg_id }}';
        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('tg_id', tgId);
        formData.append('action', 'reveal');
        formData.append('tile_index', index);
        formData.append('ajax', '1');
        formData.append('csrfmiddlewaretoken', '{{ csrf_token }}');

        try {
            const resp = await fetch("{% url 'economy:mines' %}", {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData,
                headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' }
            });
            const data = await resp.json();

            if (!data.success) return;

            const tile = document.getElementById(`tile-${index}`);
            const hiddenCover = tile.querySelector('.tile-hidden');

            if (data.status === 'bust') {
                // EXPLOSIÓN (BOMBA)
                playBombSound();
                try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.notificationOccurred('error'); } catch(e) {}
                isGameRunning = false;

                hiddenCover.classList.add('hidden');
                tile.className = "mine-tile relative rounded-xl bg-red-600 border-2 border-red-300 flex items-center justify-center text-2xl shadow-[0_0_20px_rgba(239,68,68,0.8)] animate-bounce";
                tile.querySelector('.tile-icon').innerText = '💣';

                // Revelar todas las demás minas
                data.all_mines.forEach(mIdx => {
                    const mTile = document.getElementById(`tile-${mIdx}`);
                    mTile.querySelector('.tile-hidden').classList.add('hidden');
                    mTile.className = "mine-tile relative rounded-xl bg-neutral-900 border border-red-500/50 flex items-center justify-center text-lg";
                    mTile.querySelector('.tile-icon').innerText = '💣';
                });

                document.getElementById('mines-result-box').innerHTML = `
                    <div class="p-2 bg-rose-950/90 border border-rose-600 text-rose-200 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl w-full">
                        💥 ¡BOOM! Has tocado una mina. Pierdes la apuesta.
                    </div>`;

                document.getElementById('cashout-btn').classList.add('hidden');
                document.getElementById('status-tag').classList.remove('hidden');
                document.getElementById('status-label').innerText = 'Derrota';
                document.getElementById('mines-setup-controls').classList.remove('hidden');

            } else {
                // GEMA ENCONTRADA
                revealedGems.push(index);
                playGemSound(revealedGems.length);
                try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.impactOccurred('light'); } catch(e) {}

                hiddenCover.classList.add('hidden');
                tile.className = "mine-tile relative rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-600 to-green-950 border-2 border-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center text-2xl shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(16,185,129,0.6)]";
                tile.querySelector('.tile-icon').innerText = '💎';

                document.getElementById('mult-val').innerText = Number(data.multiplier).toFixed(2);
                document.getElementById('cashout-val').innerText = data.current_cashout;

                if (data.status === 'win_full') {
                    // Limpió todo el tablero
                    playCoins();
                    isGameRunning = false;
                    document.getElementById('mines-balance').innerText = data.nuevo_saldo;
                    document.getElementById('mines-result-box').innerHTML = `
                        <div class="p-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-500 to-amber-500 text-black text-xs font-black uppercase rounded-xl w-full animate-bounce">
                            👑 ¡PERFECTO! Limpiaste todas las gemas (+${data.current_cashout} 🪙).
                        </div>`;
                    document.getElementById('cashout-btn').classList.add('hidden');
                    document.getElementById('mines-setup-controls').classList.remove('hidden');
                } else {
                    document.getElementById('cashout-btn').classList.remove('hidden');
                    document.getElementById('status-tag').classList.add('hidden');
                }
            }

        } catch(e) {
            alert("Error de conexión al abrir cofre.");
        }
    }

    async function cashoutMines() {
        if (!isGameRunning) return;

        const tgId = (typeof window.getGlobalTgId === 'function') ? window.getGlobalTgId() : '{{ tg_id }}';
        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('tg_id', tgId);
        formData.append('action', 'cashout');
        formData.append('ajax', '1');
        formData.append('csrfmiddlewaretoken', '{{ csrf_token }}');

        try {
            const resp = await fetch("{% url 'economy:mines' %}", {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData,
                headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' }
            });
            const data = await resp.json();

            if (!data.success) return;

            isGameRunning = false;
            playCoins();
            try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.notificationOccurred('success'); } catch(e) {}

            document.getElementById('mines-balance').innerText = data.nuevo_saldo;

            // Mostrar dónde estaban las minas restantes
            data.all_mines.forEach(mIdx => {
                const mTile = document.getElementById(`tile-${mIdx}`);
                mTile.querySelector('.tile-hidden').classList.add('hidden');
                mTile.className = "mine-tile relative rounded-xl bg-neutral-900 border border-neutral-700 flex items-center justify-center text-sm opacity-50";
                mTile.querySelector('.tile-icon').innerText = '💣';
            });

            document.getElementById('cashout-btn').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('status-tag').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('status-label').innerText = 'Retirado';

            document.getElementById('mines-result-box').innerHTML = `
                <div class="p-2.5 bg-emerald-950/90 border border-emerald-500 text-emerald-200 text-xs font-bold rounded-xl w-full shadow-[0_0_15px_rgba(16,185,129,0.4)] animate-bounce">
                    🎉 ¡Te retiraste con éxito! Has cobrado +${data.ganancia} 🪙 (x${Number(data.multiplier).toFixed(2)}).
                </div>`;

            document.getElementById('mines-setup-controls').classList.remove('hidden');

        } catch(e) {
            alert("Error al cobrar.");
        }
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<!-- SINTETIZADOR DE AUDIO CASINO VIP (Sin dependencias externas) -->
<script>
    const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    let audioCtx = null;

    function getAudioCtx() {
        if (!audioCtx) audioCtx = new AudioCtx();
        if (audioCtx.state === 'suspended') audioCtx.resume();
        return audioCtx;
    }

    // Sonido de clic mecánico de rodillo
    function playClack() {
        try {
            const ctx = getAudioCtx();
            const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
            const gain = ctx.createGain();
            osc.type = 'triangle';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(220, ctx.currentTime);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(60, ctx.currentTime + 0.08);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.5, ctx.currentTime);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.08);
            osc.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(ctx.destination);
            osc.start();
            osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.08);
        } catch(e) {}
    }

    // Sonido de tintineo de monedas de oro
    function playCoins() {
        try {
            const ctx = getAudioCtx();
            [987, 1318, 1567, 2093].forEach((freq, idx) => {
                const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
                const gain = ctx.createGain();
                osc.type = 'sine';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, ctx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08));
                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.3, ctx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08));
                gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08) + 0.25);
                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(ctx.destination);
                osc.start(ctx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08));
                osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08) + 0.25);
            });
        } catch(e) {}
    }
</script>

<style>
    .spinning-reel {
        animation: spinBlur 0.08s infinite linear;
        filter: blur(2px);
    }
    @keyframes spinBlur {
        0% { transform: translateY(-35px) scale(0.9); opacity: 0.5; }
        50% { transform: translateY(0px) scale(1.05); opacity: 1; }
        100% { transform: translateY(35px) scale(0.9); opacity: 0.5; }
    }
    .reel-stop {
        animation: stopBounce 0.28s ease-out;
    }
    @keyframes stopBounce {
        0% { transform: translateY(-25px); }
        60% { transform: translateY(8px); }
        100% { transform: translateY(0); }
    }
</style>

<script>
    const SYMBOLS = ['👑', '💎', '⭐', '🍇', '🍒', '🪙'];
    let isSpinning = false;

    function setBet(val) {
        if (!isSpinning) document.getElementById('bet_amount_input').value = val;
    }

    async function spinSlots() {
        if (isSpinning) return;
        const betInput = document.getElementById('bet_amount_input');
        const betVal = parseInt(betInput.value, 10);
        const resultBox = document.getElementById('result-box');
        const btn = document.getElementById('spin-btn');
        const balanceElem = document.getElementById('slots-balance');

        if (!betVal || betVal <= 0) {
            resultBox.innerHTML = '<span class="text-xs text-red-400 font-bold">⚠️ Apuesta inválida</span>';
            return;
        }

        const tgId = (typeof window.getGlobalTgId === 'function') ? window.getGlobalTgId() : '{{ tg_id }}';
        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('tg_id', tgId);
        formData.append('bet_amount', betVal);
        formData.append('ajax', '1');
        formData.append('csrfmiddlewaretoken', '{{ csrf_token }}');

        isSpinning = true;
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.classList.add('opacity-50');
        btn.innerHTML = '<span>⏳</span> Rodillos Girando...';

        resultBox.innerHTML = '<span class="text-xs text-yellow-300 font-bold animate-pulse">Rodillos a toda velocidad...</span>';

        const r1 = document.getElementById('reel-1');
        const r2 = document.getElementById('reel-2');
        const r3 = document.getElementById('reel-3');

        r1.classList.add('spinning-reel');
        r2.classList.add('spinning-reel');
        r3.classList.add('spinning-reel');

        try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.impactOccurred('heavy'); } catch(e) {}

        const int1 = setInterval(() => { r1.innerText = SYMBOLS[Math.floor(Math.random()*SYMBOLS.length)]; }, 70);
        const int2 = setInterval(() => { r2.innerText = SYMBOLS[Math.floor(Math.random()*SYMBOLS.length)]; }, 70);
        const int3 = setInterval(() => { r3.innerText = SYMBOLS[Math.floor(Math.random()*SYMBOLS.length)]; }, 70);

        try {
            const resp = await fetch("{% url 'economy:slots' %}", {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData,
                headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' }
            });
            const data = await resp.json();

            if (!data.success) {
                clearInterval(int1); clearInterval(int2); clearInterval(int3);
                r1.classList.remove('spinning-reel'); r2.classList.remove('spinning-reel'); r3.classList.remove('spinning-reel');
                isSpinning = false;
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.classList.remove('opacity-50');
                btn.innerHTML = '<span>🎰</span> Bajar Palanca (Tirar)';
                resultBox.innerHTML = `<span class="text-xs text-red-400 font-bold">❌ ${data.error}</span>`;
                return;
            }

            // Freno 1: 0.6s
            setTimeout(() => {
                clearInterval(int1);
                r1.classList.remove('spinning-reel');
                r1.innerText = data.reels[0];
                r1.classList.add('reel-stop');
                playClack();
                try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.impactOccurred('medium'); } catch(e) {}
            }, 600);

            // Freno 2: 1.1s
            setTimeout(() => {
                clearInterval(int2);
                r2.classList.remove('spinning-reel');
                r2.innerText = data.reels[1];
                r2.classList.add('reel-stop');
                playClack();
                try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.impactOccurred('medium'); } catch(e) {}
            }, 1100);

            // Freno 3: 1.6s (Final)
            setTimeout(() => {
                clearInterval(int3);
                r3.classList.remove('spinning-reel');
                r3.innerText = data.reels[2];
                r3.classList.add('reel-stop');
                playClack();

                isSpinning = false;
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.classList.remove('opacity-50');
                btn.innerHTML = '<span>🎰</span> Bajar Palanca (Tirar)';

                if (balanceElem) balanceElem.innerText = data.nuevo_saldo;

                if (data.resultado === 'jackpot') {
                    playCoins();
                    try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.notificationOccurred('success'); } catch(e) {}
                    resultBox.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="p-3 bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-950 via-yellow-600 to-yellow-950 border border-yellow-300 rounded-xl text-black text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider w-full animate-bounce shadow-lg">
                            👑 ¡JACKPOT IMPERIAL! Has ganado +${data.ganancia} 🪙 de oro.
                        </div>`;
                } else if (data.resultado === 'pair') {
                    playCoins();
                    try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.notificationOccurred('success'); } catch(e) {}
                    resultBox.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="p-2.5 bg-purple-950/80 border border-purple-500 rounded-xl text-purple-200 text-xs font-bold w-full">
                            ✨ ¡Par conseguido! Recuperas tu apuesta con ganancia (+${data.ganancia} 🪙).
                        </div>`;
                } else {
                    try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.notificationOccurred('error'); } catch(e) {}
                    resultBox.innerHTML = `
                        <div class="p-2.5 bg-rose-950/80 border border-rose-500 rounded-xl text-rose-200 text-xs font-bold w-full">
                            💀 Sin coincidencia. Pierdes ${betVal} 🪙 de oro.
                        </div>`;
                }

            }, 1600);

        } catch(e) {
            clearInterval(int1); clearInterval(int2); clearInterval(int3);
            isSpinning = false;
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.classList.remove('opacity-50');
            btn.innerHTML = '<span>🎰</span> Bajar Palanca (Tirar)';
            resultBox.innerHTML = '<span class="text-xs text-red-400 font-bold">⚠️ Error de conexión</span>';
        }
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function spinDailyWheel() {
        const btn = document.getElementById('btn-spin-daily');
        const wheel = document.getElementById('daily-wheel');
        if (!btn || !wheel) return;

        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.innerHTML = '<span>⏳</span> Girando el Destino...';
        btn.classList.add('opacity-60');

        const degrees = 1800 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 360);
        wheel.style.transform = `rotate(${degrees}deg)`;

        setTimeout(() => {
            document.getElementById('daily-wheel-form')?.submit();
        }, 3000);
    }

    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
        const rawSeconds = "{{ wallet.seconds_until_next_bonus|default:'0' }}";
        let remainingSeconds = parseInt(rawSeconds, 10) || 0;
        const timerElem = document.getElementById('countdown-timer');

        if (timerElem && remainingSeconds > 0) {
            function updateTimer() {
                if (remainingSeconds <= 0) {
                    timerElem.innerText = "¡Disponible!";
                    location.reload();
                    return;
                }
                const hours = Math.floor(remainingSeconds / 3600);
                const minutes = Math.floor((remainingSeconds % 3600) / 60);
                const seconds = remainingSeconds % 60;
                timerElem.innerText = `${hours}h ${minutes}m ${seconds}s`;
                remainingSeconds--;
            }
            updateTimer();
            setInterval(updateTimer, 1000);
        }
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function switchTab(tab) {
        document.getElementById('tab-subir').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('tab-cajas').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('tab-cartas').classList.add('hidden');

        document.getElementById('tab-btn-subir').className = "py-2 rounded-xl text-stone-400 hover:text-white";
        document.getElementById('tab-btn-cajas').className = "py-2 rounded-xl text-stone-400 hover:text-white";
        document.getElementById('tab-btn-cartas').className = "py-2 rounded-xl text-stone-400 hover:text-white";

        document.getElementById(`tab-${tab}`).classList.remove('hidden');
        document.getElementById(`tab-btn-${tab}`).className = "py-2 rounded-xl bg-vip-gold text-black font-black";
    }

    function openEditCardModal(cardId, name, idolName, rarity, boxId) {
        document.getElementById('modal-card-id').value = cardId;
        document.getElementById('modal-card-name').value = name;
        document.getElementById('modal-idol-name').value = idolName;
        document.getElementById('modal-card-rarity').value = rarity;
        document.getElementById('modal-box-id').value = boxId;
        document.getElementById('edit-card-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeEditCardModal() {
        document.getElementById('edit-card-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    }
</script>

        </div>

            <span class="block text-3xl mb-2 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">🎭</span>
            <span class="block font-bold text-base uppercase tracking-wider font-serif">Ser una Idol Real</span>

<script>
    function elegirRol(rolElegido) {
        let tgId = document.getElementById('real_tg_id').value;
        const tgApp = window.Telegram?.WebApp;
        
        // Si no venía en el formulario, tomarlo de Telegram en vivo
        if (!tgId || tgId === 'None' || tgId === 'undefined' || tgId === '123456789') {
            const user = tgApp?.initDataUnsafe?.user;
            if (user?.id) {
                tgId = String(user.id);
            } else if (typeof window.getGlobalTgId === 'function') {
                tgId = window.getGlobalTgId();
            }
            document.getElementById('real_tg_id').value = tgId;
        }
        
        document.getElementById('selected_role').value = rolElegido;
        document.getElementById('roleForm').submit();
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function togglePriceField() {
        const net = document.getElementById('network-select').value;
        const cont = document.getElementById('price-container');
        if (net === 'fans') {
            cont.classList.remove('hidden');
        } else {
            cont.classList.add('hidden');
        }
    }
</script>

<script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
        const idInput = document.getElementById('detail_tg_id');
        const nameInput = document.getElementById('detail_tg_username');
        const realId = (typeof window.getGlobalTgId === 'function') ? window.getGlobalTgId() : '123456789';
        
        if (idInput && (!idInput.value || idInput.value === 'None')) {
            idInput.value = realId;
        }

        const tg = window.Telegram?.WebApp;
        const user = tg?.initDataUnsafe?.user;
        if (nameInput && (!nameInput.value || nameInput.value === 'None')) {
            nameInput.value = user?.username ? `@${user.username}` : (user?.first_name || `Noble_${realId}`);
        }
    });

    function contactIdol(rawUsername, fallbackId) {
        let cleanUser = String(rawUsername || '').replace('@', '').trim();

        // 1. Si no tiene @username público, abrir chat directo por su ID de Telegram
        if (!cleanUser || cleanUser === 'None' || cleanUser === 'undefined' || cleanUser === 'MusaReal') {
            if (fallbackId) {
                window.location.href = `tg://user?id=${fallbackId}`;
                return;
            }
            alert("Esta Musa no tiene un enlace de contacto activo.");
            return;
        }

        // 2. Si es numérico
        if (/^\d+$/.test(cleanUser)) {
            window.location.href = `tg://user?id=${cleanUser}`;
            return;
        }

        // 3. Si tiene @username real
        const tgWebUrl = `https://t.me/${cleanUser}`;
        if (window.Telegram?.WebApp?.openTelegramLink) {
            try {
                window.Telegram.WebApp.openTelegramLink(tgWebUrl);
                return;
            } catch(e) {}
        }

        window.location.href = `tg://resolve?domain=${cleanUser}`;
        setTimeout(() => { window.open(tgWebUrl, '_blank'); }, 500);
    }

    function toggleCustomRequestModal() {
        const modal = document.getElementById('custom-request-modal');
        modal.classList.toggle('hidden');
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function togglePriceField() {
        const net = document.getElementById('network-select').value;
        const cont = document.getElementById('price-container');
        if (net === 'fans') {
            cont.classList.remove('hidden');
        } else {
            cont.classList.add('hidden');
        }
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

                            Enviar

<script>
    async function handleLike(postId) {
        try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.impactOccurred('medium'); } catch(e) {}
        const tgId = (typeof window.getGlobalTgId === 'function') ? window.getGlobalTgId() : '{{ tg_id }}';
        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('tg_id', tgId);
        formData.append('csrfmiddlewaretoken', '{{ csrf_token }}');

        try {
            const resp = await fetch(`/idols/feed/like/${postId}/`, { method: 'POST', body: formData });
            const data = await resp.json();
            const btn = document.getElementById(`like-btn-${postId}`);
            const icon = document.getElementById(`like-icon-${postId}`);
            const count = document.getElementById(`like-count-${postId}`);

            if (data.liked) {
                icon.innerText = '❤️';
                btn.className = "flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold transition-all active:scale-125 select-none text-rose-500";
            } else {
                icon.innerText = '🤍';
                btn.className = "flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm font-bold transition-all active:scale-125 select-none text-stone-400 hover:text-rose-400";
            }
            count.innerText = data.likes;
        } catch(e) {}
    }

    function toggleComments(postId) {
        const section = document.getElementById(`comments-section-${postId}`);
        if (section) section.classList.toggle('hidden');
    }

    function filterFeed(type) {
        const items = document.querySelectorAll('.feed-item');
        const btnAll = document.getElementById('btn-filter-all');
        const btnVip = document.getElementById('btn-filter-vip');

        if (type === 'vip') {
            btnVip.className = "py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider transition-all bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-800 to-fuchsia-700 text-white shadow";
            btnAll.className = "py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-stone-400 hover:text-white transition-all";
            items.forEach(el => { el.style.display = (el.dataset.network === 'fans') ? 'block' : 'none'; });
        } else {
            btnAll.className = "py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider transition-all bg-vip-gold text-black shadow";
            btnVip.className = "py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-stone-400 hover:text-white transition-all";
            items.forEach(el => el.style.display = 'block');
        }
    }

    function openTipModal(postId, idolName) {
        document.getElementById('tip-form').action = `/idols/feed/tip/${postId}/`;
        document.getElementById('tip-idol-target').innerText = `Brinda con ${idolName} (80% neto para su Bóveda).`;
        document.getElementById('tip-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeTipModal() { document.getElementById('tip-modal').classList.add('hidden'); }
    function setTipAmount(val) { document.getElementById('tip-amount-input').value = val; }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
        const idElem = document.getElementById('hidden_tg_id');
        const userElem = document.getElementById('hidden_tg_username');
        const tg = window.Telegram?.WebApp;
        
        if (!idElem.value || idElem.value === 'None') {
            idElem.value = (typeof window.getGlobalTgId === 'function') ? window.getGlobalTgId() : '123456789';
        }

        if (!userElem.value || userElem.value === 'None' || userElem.value.trim() === '') {
            const u = tg?.initDataUnsafe?.user;
            if (u) {
                userElem.value = u.username ? `@${u.username}` : (u.first_name || `Musa_${idElem.value}`);
            }
        }
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function filterGallery(type) {
        const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.idol-card');
        ['all', 'online', 'top', 'trending'].forEach(t => {
            const btn = document.getElementById(`g-filter-${t}`);
            if (btn) btn.className = "py-1.5 rounded-xl text-stone-400 hover:text-white";
        });
        document.getElementById(`g-filter-${type}`).className = "py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-vip-gold text-black font-black";

        cards.forEach(c => {
            if (type === 'all') c.style.display = 'block';
            else if (type === 'online') c.style.display = (c.dataset.status === 'online' || c.dataset.status === 'antojos') ? 'block' : 'none';
            else if (type === 'top') c.style.display = (c.dataset.top === '1') ? 'block' : 'none';
            else if (type === 'trending') c.style.display = (c.dataset.trending === '1') ? 'block' : 'none';
        });
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function filterAlbum(rarity) {
        const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.album-card');
        const btns = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn');

        btns.forEach(b => { b.className = "py-1.5 rounded-xl text-stone-400 hover:text-white filter-btn"; });
        document.getElementById(`f-${rarity}`).className = "py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-vip-gold text-black font-bold filter-btn";

        cards.forEach(c => {
            c.style.display = (rarity === 'all' || c.getAttribute('data-rarity') === rarity) ? 'flex' : 'none';
        });
    }

    function openCardActionModal(cardId, name, idolName, rarityDisplay, colorHex, imgUrl, isForSale, salePrice) {
        document.getElementById('modal-card-title').innerText = name;
        document.getElementById('modal-card-idol').innerText = `Idol: ${idolName}`;
        document.getElementById('modal-card-rarity').innerText = rarityDisplay;
        document.getElementById('modal-card-rarity').style.borderColor = colorHex;
        document.getElementById('modal-card-rarity').style.color = colorHex;
        document.getElementById('modal-card-img').src = imgUrl;
        document.getElementById('modal-card-box').style.borderColor = colorHex;
        document.getElementById('modal-card-box').style.boxShadow = `0 0 25px ${colorHex}`;

        const sellForm = document.getElementById('sell-form');
        const tradeForm = document.getElementById('trade-form');
        const cancelForm = document.getElementById('cancel-sale-form');

        sellForm.action = `/idols/photocards/sell/${cardId}/`;
        tradeForm.action = `/idols/photocards/trade/create/${cardId}/`;
        cancelForm.action = `/idols/photocards/sell/${cardId}/`;

        if (isForSale) {
            document.getElementById('modal-is-for-sale-box').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('modal-actions-box').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('modal-sale-price-label').innerText = salePrice;
        } else {
            document.getElementById('modal-is-for-sale-box').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('modal-actions-box').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        document.getElementById('card-action-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeCardActionModal() {
        document.getElementById('card-action-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

<script>
    function switchMarketTab(tab) {
        const cardsTab = document.getElementById('market-cards-tab');
        const tradesTab = document.getElementById('market-trades-tab');
        const btnCards = document.getElementById('tab-btn-cards');
        const btnTrades = document.getElementById('tab-btn-trades');

        if (tab === 'cards') {
            cardsTab.classList.remove('hidden');
            tradesTab.classList.add('hidden');
            btnCards.className = "py-2 rounded-xl bg-vip-gold text-black font-black";
            btnTrades.className = "py-2 rounded-xl text-stone-400 hover:text-white";
        } else {
            cardsTab.classList.add('hidden');
            tradesTab.classList.remove('hidden');
            btnTrades.className = "py-2 rounded-xl bg-vip-gold text-black font-black";
            btnCards.className = "py-2 rounded-xl text-stone-400 hover:text-white";
        }
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

                    <div class="absolute -top-1 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 text-rose-500 text-[10px]">▼</div>

<!-- SINTETIZADOR DE AUDIO CASINO/CS2 (Ticks progresivos y fanfarria) -->
<script>
    const AudioCtx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    let csAudioCtx = null;

    function playCSTick() {
        try {
            if (!csAudioCtx) csAudioCtx = new AudioCtx();
            if (csAudioCtx.state === 'suspended') csAudioCtx.resume();
            const osc = csAudioCtx.createOscillator();
            const gain = csAudioCtx.createGain();
            osc.type = 'triangle';
            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(320, csAudioCtx.currentTime);
            osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(70, csAudioCtx.currentTime + 0.035);
            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.25, csAudioCtx.currentTime);
            gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, csAudioCtx.currentTime + 0.035);
            osc.connect(gain);
            gain.connect(csAudioCtx.destination);
            osc.start();
            osc.stop(csAudioCtx.currentTime + 0.035);
        } catch(e) {}
    }

    function playWinFanfare(rarity) {
        try {
            if (!csAudioCtx) csAudioCtx = new AudioCtx();
            const freqs = (rarity === 'legendary') 
                ? [523, 659, 783, 1046, 1318] 
                : (rarity === 'epic' ? [440, 554, 659, 880] : [392, 523, 659]);

            freqs.forEach((freq, idx) => {
                const osc = csAudioCtx.createOscillator();
                const gain = csAudioCtx.createGain();
                osc.type = 'sine';
                osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, csAudioCtx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08));
                gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.35, csAudioCtx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08));
                gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, csAudioCtx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08) + 0.35);
                osc.connect(gain);
                gain.connect(csAudioCtx.destination);
                osc.start(csAudioCtx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08));
                osc.stop(csAudioCtx.currentTime + (idx * 0.08) + 0.35);
            });
        } catch(e) {}
    }
</script>

<script>
    let activeBoxId = null;
    let activeBoxName = '';
    let activeBoxPrice = 0;
    let isOpening = false;

    function startCaseOpening(boxId, boxName, boxPrice) {
        activeBoxId = boxId;
        activeBoxName = boxName;
        activeBoxPrice = boxPrice;

        document.getElementById('cs-box-title').innerText = boxName;
        document.getElementById('cs-roulette-phase').classList.remove('hidden');
        document.getElementById('cs-reveal-phase').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('cs-close-btn').style.display = 'block';

        const btn = document.getElementById('cs-confirm-btn');
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.innerHTML = `<span>🗝️</span> <span>Girar Ruleta (${boxPrice} 🪙)</span>`;

        const reel = document.getElementById('cs-reel');
        reel.style.transition = 'none';
        reel.style.transform = 'translateX(0px)';
        reel.innerHTML = '<span class="text-xs text-stone-500 italic py-12 px-6">Pulsa Girar para activar la ruleta...</span>';

        document.getElementById('cs-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function reopenCurrentBox() {
        startCaseOpening(activeBoxId, activeBoxName, activeBoxPrice);
    }

    function closeCSModal() {
        if (isOpening) return;
        document.getElementById('cs-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    async function confirmOpenCase() {
        if (isOpening) return;

        const tgId = (typeof window.getGlobalTgId === 'function') ? window.getGlobalTgId() : '{{ tg_id }}';
        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('tg_id', tgId);
        formData.append('csrfmiddlewaretoken', '{{ csrf_token }}');

        const btn = document.getElementById('cs-confirm-btn');
        const closeBtn = document.getElementById('cs-close-btn');
        const reel = document.getElementById('cs-reel');
        const viewport = document.getElementById('cs-viewport');

        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.innerHTML = '<span>⏳</span> Abriendo Caja...';
        closeBtn.style.display = 'none';
        isOpening = true;

        try {
            const resp = await fetch(`/idols/photocards/open/${activeBoxId}/`, {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData
            });
            const data = await resp.json();

            if (!data.success) {
                alert(data.error);
                btn.disabled = false;
                btn.innerHTML = `<span>🗝️</span> <span>Girar Ruleta (${activeBoxPrice} 🪙)</span>`;
                closeBtn.style.display = 'block';
                isOpening = false;
                return;
            }

            const balanceElem = document.getElementById('user-gold-balance');
            if (balanceElem) balanceElem.innerText = data.nuevo_saldo;

            // Renderizar las 35 cartas con proporciones CS2
            reel.style.transition = 'none';
            reel.style.transform = 'translateX(0px)';
            reel.innerHTML = data.reel.map(c => `
                <div class="w-[110px] h-[150px] rounded-xl flex-shrink-0 bg-[#12071d] border-2 flex flex-col justify-between p-1.5 relative overflow-hidden select-none shadow-md"
                     style="border-color: ${c.color};">
                    <img src="${c.image_url}" class="w-full h-[100px] object-cover rounded-lg">
                    <div class="text-center flex flex-col mt-1">
                        <span class="text-[9px] font-black text-white truncate">${c.name}</span>
                        <span class="text-[8px] font-mono uppercase font-bold" style="color: ${c.color};">${c.rarity_display}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="absolute bottom-0 inset-x-0 h-1.5" style="background-color: ${c.color};"></div>
                </div>
            `).join('');

            reel.offsetHeight;

            // Cálculo exacto del centro
            const cardPitch = 120;
            const winningIndex = data.winning_index; // 28
            const containerWidth = viewport.offsetWidth;
            const cardCenter = (winningIndex * cardPitch) + 55;
            const targetX = - (cardCenter - (containerWidth / 2) + (Math.random() * 20 - 10));

            reel.style.transition = 'transform 4.8s cubic-bezier(0.1, 0.85, 0.2, 1)';
            reel.style.transform = `translateX(${targetX}px)`;

            try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.impactOccurred('heavy'); } catch(e) {}

            let ticks = 0;
            let tickDelay = 90;
            function scheduleTick() {
                if (ticks > 26) return;
                playCSTick();
                try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.impactOccurred('light'); } catch(e) {}
                ticks++;
                tickDelay += Math.floor(ticks * 5);
                setTimeout(scheduleTick, tickDelay);
            }
            scheduleTick();

            setTimeout(() => {
                isOpening = false;
                closeBtn.style.display = 'block';

                playWinFanfare(data.winner.rarity);
                try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.notificationOccurred('success'); } catch(e) {}

                document.getElementById('cs-roulette-phase').classList.add('hidden');

                const revealPhase = document.getElementById('cs-reveal-phase');
                const winnerBox = document.getElementById('cs-card-reveal-box');
                const winnerImg = document.getElementById('cs-winner-img');
                const winnerName = document.getElementById('cs-winner-name');
                const winnerIdol = document.getElementById('cs-winner-idol');
                const winnerRarity = document.getElementById('cs-winner-rarity');
                const glow = document.getElementById('cs-reveal-glow');

                winnerImg.src = data.winner.image_url;
                winnerName.innerText = data.winner.name;
                winnerIdol.innerText = `Idol: ${data.winner.idol_name}`;

                winnerRarity.innerText = data.winner.rarity_display;
                winnerRarity.style.borderColor = data.winner.color;
                winnerRarity.style.color = data.winner.color;
                winnerRarity.style.backgroundColor = `${data.winner.color}22`;

                winnerBox.style.borderColor = data.winner.color;
                winnerBox.style.boxShadow = `0 0 35px ${data.winner.color}`;
                glow.style.backgroundColor = data.winner.color;

                revealPhase.classList.remove('hidden');

                setTimeout(() => {
                    winnerBox.style.opacity = '1';
                    winnerBox.style.transform = 'scale(1)';
                }, 50);

            }, 4800);

        } catch(e) {
            alert("Error de conexión al abrir la caja.");
            btn.disabled = false;
            closeBtn.style.display = 'block';
            isOpening = false;
        }
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

            <div class="text-left">
                <label class="block text-[11px] text-vip-gold font-bold uppercase mb-1">Nombre Sagrado de la Criatura</label>
                <input type="text" name="name" required placeholder="Ej: Nyx, Kitsuray, Drakon, Fenris..." 
                       class="w-full bg-black/80 border border-vip-gold/40 rounded-xl p-3 text-xs text-white focus:outline-none focus:border-vip-gold">

<!-- SINTETIZADOR DE AUDIO CASINO/RPG WEB AUDIO API -->
<script>
    const AudioContextClass = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
    let sanctuaryAudioCtx = null;

    function getSanctuaryAudio() {
        if (!sanctuaryAudioCtx) sanctuaryAudioCtx = new AudioContextClass();
        if (sanctuaryAudioCtx.state === 'suspended') sanctuaryAudioCtx.resume();
        return sanctuaryAudioCtx;
    }

    function playSoundEffect(type) {
        try {
            const ctx = getSanctuaryAudio();
            if (type === 'feed') {
                [330, 440, 550].forEach((freq, idx) => {
                    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
                    const gain = ctx.createGain();
                    osc.type = 'sine';
                    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.07);
                    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.07);
                    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.07 + 0.15);
                    osc.connect(gain);
                    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
                    osc.start(ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.07);
                    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.07 + 0.15);
                });
            } else if (type === 'pet') {
                [659, 880, 987].forEach((freq, idx) => {
                    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
                    const gain = ctx.createGain();
                    osc.type = 'sine';
                    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.08);
                    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.25, ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.08);
                    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.08 + 0.25);
                    osc.connect(gain);
                    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
                    osc.start(ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.08);
                    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.08 + 0.25);
                });
            } else if (type === 'evolve') {
                [523, 659, 783, 1046, 1318].forEach((freq, idx) => {
                    const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
                    const gain = ctx.createGain();
                    osc.type = 'triangle';
                    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.1);
                    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.35, ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.1);
                    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.1 + 0.5);
                    osc.connect(gain);
                    gain.connect(ctx.destination);
                    osc.start(ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.1);
                    osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + idx * 0.1 + 0.5);
                });
            }
        } catch(e) {}
    }

    function switchSanctuaryTab(tabName) {
        const tabExpeditions = document.getElementById('tab-expeditions');
        const tabSpecies = document.getElementById('tab-species');
        const btnExp = document.getElementById('tab-btn-expeditions');
        const btnSpec = document.getElementById('tab-btn-species');

        if (tabName === 'expeditions') {
            tabExpeditions.classList.remove('hidden');
            tabSpecies.classList.add('hidden');
            btnExp.className = "py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-vip-gold text-black font-black flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow";
            btnSpec.className = "py-2.5 rounded-xl text-stone-400 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all";
        } else {
            tabExpeditions.classList.add('hidden');
            tabSpecies.classList.remove('hidden');
            btnSpec.className = "py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-vip-gold text-black font-black flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 shadow";
            btnExp.className = "py-2.5 rounded-xl text-stone-400 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-all";
        }
    }

    function triggerPetTouch(e) {
        spawnFloatingHeart(e.clientX, e.clientY);
        ajaxInteractPet('pet');
    }

    function spawnFloatingHeart(x, y) {
        const heart = document.createElement('div');
        heart.innerText = '💖 +15 XP';
        heart.className = 'fixed pointer-events-none z-50 text-xs font-black text-pink-300 drop-shadow-[0_0_8px_rgba(244,114,182,0.9)]';
        heart.style.left = `${(x || window.innerWidth / 2) - 25}px`;
        heart.style.top = `${(y || window.innerHeight / 2) - 20}px`;
        heart.style.transition = 'all 1s cubic-bezier(0.1, 0.8, 0.2, 1)';
        document.body.appendChild(heart);

        setTimeout(() => {
            heart.style.transform = 'translateY(-50px) scale(1.15)';
            heart.style.opacity = '0';
        }, 20);

        setTimeout(() => { heart.remove(); }, 1050);
    }

    async function ajaxInteractPet(action) {
        const tgId = (typeof window.getGlobalTgId === 'function') ? window.getGlobalTgId() : '{{ tg_id }}';
        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('tg_id', tgId);
        formData.append('action', action);
        formData.append('ajax', '1');
        formData.append('csrfmiddlewaretoken', '{{ csrf_token }}');

        try {
            const resp = await fetch("{% url 'pets:interact' %}", {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData,
                headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' }
            });
            const data = await resp.json();

            const toastBox = document.getElementById('pet-toast-box');

            if (!data.success) {
                try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.notificationOccurred('warning'); } catch(e) {}
                toastBox.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-amber-950/90 border border-amber-500/80 text-amber-200 text-xs font-bold text-center shadow-lg">
                        ${data.error}
                    </div>
                `;
                setTimeout(() => { toastBox.innerHTML = ''; }, 3500);
                return;
            }

            playSoundEffect(action);
            try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.notificationOccurred('success'); } catch(e) {}

            document.getElementById('pet-energy-text').innerText = `${data.pet_energy}%`;
            document.getElementById('pet-energy-bar').style.width = `${data.pet_energy}%`;

            document.getElementById('pet-happiness-text').innerText = `${data.pet_happiness}%`;
            document.getElementById('pet-happiness-bar').style.width = `${data.pet_happiness}%`;

            const moodBadge = document.getElementById('pet-mood-badge');
            if (data.pet_happiness >= 80) moodBadge.innerText = '🥰 Devoto & Radiante';
            else if (data.pet_happiness >= 50) moodBadge.innerText = '😊 Alegre';
            else if (data.pet_happiness >= 25) moodBadge.innerText = '🥱 Cansado';
            else moodBadge.innerText = '🥺 Solitario';

            document.getElementById('pet-xp-text').innerText = `${data.pet_xp} / ${data.pet_xp_needed} XP`;
            document.getElementById('pet-xp-bar').style.width = `${data.pet_xp_percent}%`;
            document.getElementById('pet-level-badge').innerText = data.pet_level;

            if (data.cooldown_minutes) {
                document.getElementById('pet-action-label').innerText = `Descanso (${data.cooldown_minutes}m)`;
            }

            if (data.evolved) {
                triggerEvolutionPopup(data);
            }

            toastBox.innerHTML = `
                <div class="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-950/90 border border-emerald-500 text-emerald-200 text-xs font-bold text-center shadow-lg">
                    ${data.message}
                </div>
            `;
            setTimeout(() => { toastBox.innerHTML = ''; }, 3500);

        } catch(e) {
            console.error(e);
        }
    }

    function triggerEvolutionPopup(data) {
        playSoundEffect('evolve');
        try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.impactOccurred('heavy'); } catch(e) {}

        document.getElementById('evo-modal-name').innerText = data.new_name;
        document.getElementById('evo-modal-title').innerText = data.new_title;
        document.getElementById('evo-modal-buff').innerText = data.new_buff;
        document.getElementById('evo-modal-img').src = data.new_sprite;

        document.getElementById('pet-avatar-img').src = data.new_sprite;
        document.getElementById('pet-evolved-name').innerText = `${data.new_name}`;
        document.getElementById('pet-buff-text').innerText = data.new_buff;
        document.getElementById('pet-stage-badge').innerText = `Fase ${data.new_stage}/3: ${data.new_name}`;

        document.getElementById('evolution-modal').classList.remove('hidden');
    }

    function closeEvolutionModal() {
        document.getElementById('evolution-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    }

    let currentLootData = null;

    async function launchExpedition(zoneKey, zoneTitle) {
        const tgId = (typeof window.getGlobalTgId === 'function') ? window.getGlobalTgId() : '{{ tg_id }}';
        const formData = new FormData();
        formData.append('tg_id', tgId);
        formData.append('zone', zoneKey);
        formData.append('ajax', '1');
        formData.append('csrfmiddlewaretoken', '{{ csrf_token }}');

        try {
            const resp = await fetch("{% url 'pets:expedition' %}", {
                method: 'POST',
                body: formData,
                headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' }
            });
            const data = await resp.json();

            if (!data.success) {
                const toastBox = document.getElementById('pet-toast-box');
                toastBox.innerHTML = `
                    <div class="p-3 rounded-2xl bg-amber-950/90 border border-amber-500/80 text-amber-200 text-xs font-bold text-center shadow-lg">
                        ${data.error}
                    </div>
                `;
                setTimeout(() => { toastBox.innerHTML = ''; }, 3500);
                return;
            }

            currentLootData = data;
            document.getElementById('exp-modal-title').innerText = `Explorando ${zoneTitle}`;
            document.getElementById('exp-modal-subtitle').innerText = `Tu mascota regresó de ${zoneTitle} con un cofre antiguo:`;
            document.getElementById('chest-box').classList.remove('hidden');
            document.getElementById('loot-reveal').classList.add('hidden');
            document.getElementById('expedition-modal').classList.remove('hidden');

            try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.impactOccurred('heavy'); } catch(e) {}
        } catch(e) {
            alert("Error al iniciar expedición.");
        }
    }

    function openFoundChest() {
        if (!currentLootData) return;
        playSoundEffect('pet');
        try { window.Telegram?.WebApp?.HapticFeedback?.notificationOccurred('success'); } catch(e) {}

        document.getElementById('chest-box').classList.add('hidden');
        document.getElementById('loot-reveal').classList.remove('hidden');

        document.getElementById('loot-gold').innerText = `+${currentLootData.gold}`;
        document.getElementById('loot-xp').innerText = `+${currentLootData.xp}`;

        const foodElem = document.getElementById('loot-food');
        if (currentLootData.food_found) foodElem.classList.remove('hidden');
        else foodElem.classList.add('hidden');

        const cardBox = document.getElementById('loot-card-box');
        if (currentLootData.card_dropped) {
            const c = currentLootData.card_dropped;
            cardBox.classList.remove('hidden');
            cardBox.style.borderColor = c.color;
            document.getElementById('loot-card-img').src = c.image_url;
            document.getElementById('loot-card-name').innerText = c.name;
            document.getElementById('loot-card-idol').innerText = `Idol: ${c.idol_name}`;
            
            const badge = document.getElementById('loot-card-rarity');
            badge.innerText = c.rarity_display;
            badge.style.borderColor = c.color;
            badge.style.color = c.color;
        } else {
            cardBox.classList.add('hidden');
        }

        document.getElementById('pet-energy-text').innerText = `${currentLootData.pet_energy}%`;
        document.getElementById('pet-energy-bar').style.width = `${currentLootData.pet_energy}%`;
        document.getElementById('pet-level-badge').innerText = currentLootData.pet_level;

        if (currentLootData.evolved) {
            triggerEvolutionPopup(currentLootData);
        }
    }

    function closeExpeditionModal() {
        document.getElementById('expedition-modal').classList.add('hidden');
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}
